+/-- **The clocked universal NDTM at linear overhead** ([AB09, Exercise 2.6];
+decision CH34-Q8; spec, fill pending — phase P3.3): for every effective
+nondeterministic scheme there is a universal NDTM `UN` such that for every
+code `α` there is a constant `C` with, on input `⟨⟨bits t, α⟩, x⟩`: every
+branch of `UN` halts within `C·(t+1)` steps, and `UN` accepts within
+`C·(t+1)` iff the coded machine accepts `x` within `t`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Guess first, then interpret deterministically (user
+decision 1 of 2026-10-13; a declared sketch deviation from the P3.3 route,
+which passed choice bits through one per simulated step). `UN` parses the
+header and expands `t` into a unary block on a stream tape
+(`Turing.expandTM`). It then **guesses its whole choice word up front**: the
+bounded choice writer (`Turing.ndWriterTM_run_block`) overwrites the block
+with `t` choice bits in `t + 1` steps, the same time on every branch, and
+the stream head is rewound (`Turing.skipBackTM`). Everything after the guess
+is deterministic: the interpreter core `Turing.exists_ndInterpreterCore`,
+run on the guessed stream `s`. Per code, the core installs the serialization
+of the decoded machine's **replay machine** (`Turing.NDTM.replayTM`, the
+coded NDTM as a deterministic three-work-tape machine that reads its choices
+from the last tape; `Turing.exists_replayCanonizer`; user decision 3). It
+copies the input window `x.take t` (`Turing.exists_inputWindowCopyTM`; the
+whole `x` is never copied, so the budget need not dominate `|x|`, and input
+locality, `Turing.NDTM.runWith_initCfg_take`, makes the window enough; user
+decision 5). It then runs the deterministic clocked interpreter loop: one
+round of the step block H2, at three work tapes, per simulated step, hosted
+by Z1 on the window, with the countdown of the budget word `bits t`
+**fused into the loop** and **amortized**, not pointwise (round-1 finding
+5): a decrement from `j` borrows across `ν₂(j)` trailing zeros, unbounded
+for a single tick, but the borrows of `t` ticks sum to at most `t`
+(`Turing.counterOverhead_le_of_le`). It is not a countdown that re-scans
+the counter word at every tick, which is where the received
+`Turing.timed_universal` pays its `C·(t+1)²`, exactly what decision CH34-Q8
+forbids. Per simulated step the remaining cost is one table round,
+`O(|table|)`, a constant for fixed `α`, absorbed into `C`. The core accepts
+iff the coded machine's branch along `s.take t` halts with output `[true]`.
+The assembly is the nondeterministic phase host `Turing.NDTM.phaseHostND`
+over a deterministic prefix, the writer and the deterministic core: the
+transfer `Turing.NDTM.runWith_mapState_of_agreeOn_det` carries each
+deterministic contract to every branch, and the replay lemma
+`Turing.NDTM.replayTM_runFrom` identifies the core's simulated run with the
+coded branch. So every branch of `UN` halts within `C·(t+1)`, and `UN`
+accepts iff some guessed `s` is an accepting branch of length `t`
+(`Turing.FinNDTM.AcceptsWithin.mono` absorbs the timing slack). No
+nondeterministic counter loop is needed (statement note SC-3 dissolves): no
+choice occurs inside a multi-step round. Linear overhead (CH34-Q8) is kept:
+the expansion, the guess, the rewind and the window copy each cost
+`O(t + 1)`. Fill obligations, named: the startup and per-code installation;
+the choice writer's bounded contract; the window copy; the interpreter core
+(H1's hosted clock over the step block at three work tapes, harvested with
+H2's promotion); the guess-then-run assembly; the `C`-ledger per code. -/
+theorem exists_timed_universal_NDTM (c : EffectiveNDMachineCode) :
+    ∃ UN : FinNDTM Bool, ∀ α : List Bool, ∃ C : ℕ, ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ),
+      UN.tm.HaltsWithin (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) (C * (t + 1)) ∧
+      (UN.AcceptsWithin (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) (C * (t + 1)) ↔
+        (c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t) := by
+  exact un_universal_of_setup c un_setup
+
+/-- **Nondeterministic acceptance evaluates deterministically in exponential
+time** ([AB09, §3.2: "this trivial exponential simulation … does suffice to
+establish a hierarchy theorem"]; spec, fill pending — phase P3.3): a
+deterministic machine decides, on input `⟨⟨bits t, α⟩, x⟩`, whether the coded
+machine accepts `x` within `t`, in time `C · 2^{C·(t+1)}`. This is the flip
+at each stage top of the lazy diagonalization — the only place the answer is
+ever negated.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `BF := E(UN)` (user decision 2 of 2026-10-13; a declared
+sketch deviation from the P3.3 route, which replayed every choice word
+through the universal's deterministic core with an inter-replay cleanup):
+the exhaustive replay `Turing.FinNDTM.exists_exhaustiveDecider` of the fixed
+universal NDTM `UN` of `Turing.exists_timed_universal_NDTM`. On
+`⟨⟨bits t, α⟩, x⟩` every branch of `UN` halts within `C_α·(t+1)`, so `E`
+decides `UN`'s acceptance within that bound, which is the coded machine's
+acceptance within `t`, in time `C_E·2^{C_E·(C_α·(t+1)+1)} ≤ C·2^{C·(t+1)}`
+with `C := C_E·(C_α+1)`. This derivation is proved, as
+`Turing.ndAcceptsWithin_decider_of_universal`. Fill obligation, named: `E`
+itself (iterative deepening over the choice words of the fixed `UN`, each
+replayed by its replay machine under the step-clocked runner, with a window
+clear between replays; no `|x|` term). The proof applies
+`Turing.ndAcceptsWithin_decider_of_universal` to `Turing.exists_timed_universal_NDTM`. -/
+theorem exists_ndAcceptsWithin_decider (c : EffectiveNDMachineCode) :
+    ∃ BF : FinTM Bool, ∀ α : List Bool, ∃ C : ℕ, ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ),
+      ((c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t →
+        BF.ComputesInTime (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) [true]
+          (C * 2 ^ (C * (t + 1)))) ∧
+      (¬(c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t →
+        BF.ComputesInTime (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) [false]
+          (C * 2 ^ (C * (t + 1)))) := by
+  exact ndAcceptsWithin_decider_of_universal c (exists_timed_universal_NDTM c)
+
+end Turing
+
+namespace Complexity
+
+open Turing
+
+/-- **The nondeterministic time hierarchy theorem** ([AB09, Theorem 3.2];
+[Coo72]; spec, fill pending — phase P3.3, **the phase summit**): for
+time-constructible `f` and `g`, if every constant multiple of
+`f(n+1) + f(n) + n + 1` is eventually below `g(n)` — the book's
+`f(n+1) = o(g(n))` with the two declared addends (module docstring) — then
+`NTIME f ⊊ NTIME (g + 1)`. Book strength: the overhead on `f` is **linear**
+(decision CH34-Q8), in contrast to the received deterministic hierarchy's
+square.
+
+**Proof sketch.** *Inclusion:* the hypothesis at `A = 1` gives
+`f n ≤ g n` eventually (the `f n` addend); finitely many lengths absorb into
+the constant (the received `Complexity.time_hierarchy` `F`-sum idiom; no
+monotonicity needed). *Strictness* is lazy diagonalization [AB09, proof of
+Theorem 3.2 and Figure 3.1], over a scheme fixed by
+`Turing.exists_effectiveNDMachineCode`, rebuilt per the round-1 audit (its
+two majors: a per-code universal constant cannot be absorbed by choosing a
+padded large index — padding preserves `decode`, no law preserves cost —
+and the stage locator must be computable within the allowance). The
+repaired construction: **(i) the fixed-code stage schedule**: stage
+`i = pair(j, r)` runs the `j`-th binary string `α_j` (unranking), so every
+code recurs at infinitely many stages — no padded-index selection, and the
+alleged decider's constants stay fixed along its own stage subsequence (the
+same discipline as the P3.2 enumeration's repetition coordinate).
+**(ii) the `f`-adaptive ladder with capped comparisons**: with
+`a := ℓ_i + 1` the stage bottom, `T*_i := (f a + a + 1)²` and
+`ℓ_{i+1} := 2^{(T*_i)²}`, seeded at `ℓ₀ := 2` with the finitely many
+lengths at or below the seed rejected (round-2 finding 1 fixed the seed
+and this formula as *the* sequence — the round-2 pack's paraphrase
+differed by an offset, a pack erratum acknowledged in the resolutions) —
+an explicit recurrence over `f`'s constructibility witness; the locator
+compares `n` against `ℓ_{i+1}` by **bit-length arithmetic with capped
+witness runs** (the ladder value is
+never materialized in unary, and a capped run that fails to finish itself
+decides the comparison: an unfinished `f`-witness already certifies
+`ℓ_{i+1} > n`), with the completed stages' costs summing geometrically and
+the one incomplete evaluation capped — total `O(g n + 1)` after computing
+`g n` once by `g`'s witness. **(iii) the self-clocked mid-rung, guess first** (user decision 1 of
+2026-10-13; a declared sketch deviation from passing choice bits through):
+right after computing `g n`, `D` **guesses a stream of `g n` choice bits**
+with the bounded choice writer (`Turing.ndWriterTM_run_block`), its only
+nondeterministic phase; every later phase is deterministic. For
+`ℓ_i < n < ℓ_{i+1}`, `D` materializes `⟨⟨bits (g n), α_j⟩, 1^{n+1}⟩` and
+runs the deterministic interpreter core `Turing.exists_ndInterpreterCore`
+on it, hosted by Z1, with the guessed stream, at nominal simulated budget
+`g n`, under `D`'s **own step-clocked countdown of `K·(g n + 1)` steps**
+(the step clock `Turing.exists_stepClockTMVia`, shared with the B6 hosts)
+— `K` fixed by `D`'s architecture, independent of the code, **with the
+allowance split stated** (round-2 finding 2): a fixed share is reserved
+ahead of simulation for the one `g`-witness run (bounded by its own
+`c_g·(g n + 1)` clause — it runs *before* the countdown exists and is not
+cut by it), for the guess, for budget and countdown preparation, and for
+the worst single binary borrow; the **prefix-bound obligation is a clause
+of the core's contract**: when the simulated branch halts at `t' ≤ g n`,
+the core stops within `C_α·(t' + |x| + |bits (g n)| + 1)` (here
+`|x| = n + 1`, the bounded window copy of user decision 5), so the
+remaining share covers the relevant branches; `D` accepts iff the core
+accepts within the countdown, and branches cut by the countdown **reject**
+(sound: a cut branch is non-accepting, and completed accepting simulations
+are sound).
+**(iv) the top rung**: at `n = ℓ_{i+1}`, `D` runs `BF` on
+`⟨bits T*_i, α_j, 1^{a}⟩` under the same self-cap and **flips** a completed
+verdict (default answer if the cap trips); non-unary inputs reject.
+`D ∈ NTIME (g + 1)` **by construction**: every phase is either bounded
+within its reserved share of the `K·(g n + 1)` allowance or run under the
+step-clock cap, uniformly in the code. *The chain, for the alleged
+decider:* given `N` deciding `L(D)` within `c₀ · f`, take `M'` and `C₁`
+from `Turing.FinNDTM.exists_codeNDTM_accepts_linear N` and let `α` be
+`M'`'s code with its **fixed** interpreter constant `C_α`. Mid-rung, at the
+late stages of `α`'s subsequence: by the core's prefix clause the genuine
+simulation of an accepting `N`-branch costs at most
+`C_α·(C₁·(c₀·f(n+1) + 1) + (n + 1) + |bits (g n)| + 1)`, which is at most
+`A*·(f(n+1) + n + 1) + C_α·|bits (g n)|` host steps with
+`A* := C_α·(C₁·c₀ + C₁ + 2) + 1` **fixed**, and the domination hypothesis
+(its `f(n+1)` and `n + 1` addends; the logarithmic term is `o(g n)`) puts
+that below `K·(g n + 1)` eventually — so the countdown never cuts the
+relevant branches, and
+`D(1^n) = [M'` accepts `1^{n+1}` within the window`] = [1^{n+1} ∈ L(D)]`
+(forward by the linear transfer and `AcceptsWithin.mono`; backward by the
+unbounded transfer plus truncation at `N`'s own all-branch budget,
+`Turing.NDTM.runWith_of_halt`) — the chain (3.3). Top rung: the flip needs
+the transfer at the **bottom** length `a`:
+`T*_i = (f a + a + 1)² ≥ C₁·(c₀·f a + 1)` eventually — the square against
+fixed constants, no `g a` comparison needed; the hypothesis's `f n` addend
+serves the inclusion half (round-2 note 3) — and `BF`'s cost
+`C_BF·2^{C_BF·(T*_i + 1)} ≤ K·(g n + 1)` eventually along the subsequence
+since `g n ≥ n = 2^{(T*_i)²}` — the ladder's square in the exponent
+outruns any fixed linear exponent — which is the chain's (3.4); `N`
+agreeing with `D` on the whole stage collapses (3.3) into (3.4)'s flip,
+the contradiction of [AB09, Figure 3.1]. Fill obligations, named: the
+schedule and unranking; the ladder recurrence and the capped-comparison
+locator with its amortized ledger; the guess and the step-clocked
+interpreter core at fixed `K`; the two transfer instantiations (at `f(n+1)` mid-rung, at `f a`
+bottom); the chain induction. -/
+theorem ntime_hierarchy {f g : ℕ → ℕ} (hf : TimeConstructible f)
+    (hg : TimeConstructible g)
+    (hfg : ∀ A : ℕ, ∃ N, ∀ n ≥ N, A * (f (n + 1) + f n + n + 1) ≤ g n) :
+    NTIME f ⊂ NTIME (fun n => g n + 1) := by
+  sorry
+
+/-- **The nondeterministic time hierarchy for a positive bound**
+([AB09, Theorem 3.2]; spec, fill pending): as `Complexity.ntime_hierarchy`,
+with the larger class exactly `NTIME g` when `g` never vanishes — mirroring
+`Complexity.time_hierarchy_of_pos`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `c·(g n + 1) ≤ 2c · g n` when `g n ≥ 1`, so
+`NTIME (g + 1) ⊆ NTIME g`; the reverse is `Complexity.NTIME.mono`; conclude
+from `Complexity.ntime_hierarchy`.
+
+Formally, the factor `2` is absorbed into the membership witness's constant
+(`Complexity.mem_NTIME_iff`, `Turing.FinNDTM.decidesInTime_iff`). -/
+theorem ntime_hierarchy_of_pos {f g : ℕ → ℕ} (hf : TimeConstructible f)
+    (hg : TimeConstructible g) (hpos : ∀ n, 0 < g n)
+    (hfg : ∀ A : ℕ, ∃ N, ∀ n ≥ N, A * (f (n + 1) + f n + n + 1) ≤ g n) :
+    NTIME f ⊂ NTIME g := by
+  have hback : NTIME (fun n => g n + 1) ⊆ NTIME g := by
+    intro L hL
+    have hscale : L ∈ NTIME (fun n => 2 * g n) :=
+      NTIME.mono (fun n => by have := hpos n; omega) hL
+    obtain ⟨a, N, hN⟩ := mem_NTIME_iff.mp hscale
+    apply mem_NTIME_iff.mpr
+    refine ⟨a * 2, N, FinNDTM.decidesInTime_iff.mpr ?_⟩
+    intro x
+    simpa only [Nat.mul_assoc] using (FinNDTM.decidesInTime_iff.mp hN x)
+  have heq : NTIME (fun n => g n + 1) = NTIME g :=
+    Set.Subset.antisymm hback (NTIME.mono (fun n => Nat.le_succ (g n)))
+  simpa only [heq] using ntime_hierarchy hf hg hfg
+
+/-- **The showcase separation** (spec, fill pending — phase P3.3):
+`NTIME(n + 1) ⊊ NTIME((n + 1)²)`, the ℕ-rendering of the book's displayed
+instance `NTIME(n) ⊊ NTIME(n^{1.5})` (fractional exponents have no ℕ-valued
+normal form in the campaign; the square is the nearest constructible bound).
+This is exactly what decision CH34-Q8 buys: under the received
+**deterministic** hierarchy's quadratic overhead, `A·(2n + 2)² ≤ (n + 1)²`
+fails for every **positive** `A` (round-1 finding 9; `A = 0` holds
+vacuously), so no square-overhead route separates these two classes
+— the linear-overhead universal is load-bearing.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `Complexity.ntime_hierarchy_of_pos` at `f := n + 1`,
+`g := (n + 1)²`: positivity is immediate; domination is
+`A·((n + 2) + (n + 1) + n + 1) = A·(3n + 4) ≤ (n + 1)²` for `n ≥ 3A + 4`.
+The two constructibility witnesses are `Complexity.timeConstructible_poly` at
+`(C, c) = (1, 0)` and `(1, 1)`; domination takes `N = 3A + 4` and multiplies `N ≤ n` by `n`. -/
+theorem NTIME_linear_ssubset_square :
+    NTIME (fun n => n + 1) ⊂ NTIME (fun n => (n + 1) ^ 2) := by
+  have hf : TimeConstructible (fun n => n + 1) := by
+    have h : (fun n : ℕ => 1 * (n + 1) ^ (0 + 1)) = (fun n => n + 1) := by
+      funext n
+      simp only [Nat.zero_add, pow_one, one_mul]
+    rw [← h]
+    exact timeConstructible_poly 1 0 (by decide)
+  have hg : TimeConstructible (fun n => (n + 1) ^ 2) := by
+    have h : (fun n : ℕ => 1 * (n + 1) ^ (1 + 1)) = (fun n => (n + 1) ^ 2) := by
+      funext n
+      simp only [one_mul]
+    rw [← h]
+    exact timeConstructible_poly 1 1 (by decide)
+  apply ntime_hierarchy_of_pos hf hg
+  · intro n
+    exact pow_pos (Nat.succ_pos n) _
+  · intro A
+    refine ⟨3 * A + 4, fun n hn => ?_⟩
+    nlinarith [Nat.mul_le_mul_right n hn]
+
+end Complexity

+## The setup machine, as built
+
+**Tape layout** (`k := K + kC + 1`): the core's `K` tapes under `ι := castSucc ∘ castAdd kC`
+(so `ι jR` is the stream tape), the embedded header composite's `kC` tapes under
+`ιC := castSucc ∘ natAdd K`, and `cap := Fin.last (K + kC)` — the capture tape, which is also
+the expansion's counter tape (`expandTM` counts on the last tape). Here
+`kC := (bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM F).k` with `F := FinTM.pairExtractTM true false`,
+read only through `FinTM.bufferedCompTM_k` and `universalPrefixTM_k` as
+`kC = universalPrefixTM.k + (1 + F.k)`; **`F.k` is left abstract throughout** (see rule 10
+below). Inside the composite, the code buffer is coordinate `natAdd universalPrefixTM.k
+(castAdd F.k 0)` (val 0) and the extractor bank `natAdd universalPrefixTM.k (natAdd 1 e)`
+(val `1 + e`), both transported by `Fin.cast` along `bufferedCompTM_k`; their images are
+`iBuf` (val `K`) and the bank `Sx` (vals `K + 1 + e`).
+
+**Phases**, seam-composed left-nested (`seamCompTM`), with `P := { chain with q₀ := the
+nested-inl entry }` so that `MultiTapeTM.initCfg_with_q₀` / `runFrom_with_q₀` give the start:
+
+| # | machine | what it does | exact time |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| A1 | `embedSilentRetTM ιC cap MC.tm` | the header pass: the composite `bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM F` runs embedded and silent; its first halt (at exactly `3|u|+4+(4|bt|+5)`, `un_canon_halt`) lands in the live return anchor with the output `bits t` captured on `cap` | `3|u|+4+4|bt|+5` |
+| A2 | `rewindTM k iBuf` | code-buffer head `2|bt|+2 → 0` | `2|bt|+4` |
+| A3 | `clearTM k iBuf` | blank the code buffer's word `u` | `2|u|+2` |
+| A4 | `un_bankTM k Sx cap` | the guided bank erase: blanks every extractor tape while the guide (`cap`) head travels with the bank; leaves `cap`'s word intact with its head at the origin — the capture rewind for free | `|bt|+2` |
+| A5 | `expandTM (K+kC) iBlkb [true]` | the expansion: counter `bits t` on `cap` (value `t` by `counterValue_bits`), writes the `t`-cell block on `ι jR`; counter left all-`true` at width `|bt|`, head back at the origin | `2t + counterOverhead bt (t+1)` |
+| A6 | `rewindTM k (ι jR)` | block head `t → 0` | `t+2` |
+| A7 | `clearTM k cap` | blank the all-`true` counter word | `2|bt|+2` |
+| A8 | `un_inRewTM k` | the input rewind: input head `2|u|+3 → 1` | `2|u|+4` |
+
+`pexit := Sum.inr 2` (A8's park). Exit configuration: exactly the hypothesis's
+`⟨some pexit, 1, Function.update (fun _ _ => none) (ι jR) blk, fun _ => 0, []⟩` with
+`blk := FinTM.bufferTape (List.replicate t true)` (`blk p ≠ none ↔ 0 ≤ p < t` proved from
+`bufferTape_nat`/`bufferTape_of_neg`/`length_replicate`). First exit: every phase's framed run
+carries a first-visit cut, inherited seam by seam (`seamCompTM_firstReturn_ofCfg` via
+`un_step`/`un_step'`).

+**The ledger.** `TP = Σ Tᵢ + 7 ≤ 25|bt| + 7|α| + 7t + 48 + (counterOverhead ≤ 4t + 2|bt| + 2
+by counterOverhead_le)`, and `|bt| ≤ t` (`length_bits_le_self`), so `TP ≤ 32t + 7|α| + 50 ≤
+CP·(t+1)` with **`CP := 7·|α| + 130`** — no `|x|` term (the header pass never reads past the
+header; the input rewind costs `2|u|+4`, and `|u| = 2|bt|+2+|α|` by `length_pairEncode`).
+Edge cases hold uniformly: `t = 0` (`bits 0 = []`, empty block, `blk` everywhere blank),
+`x = []` (A8's unconditional first left step handles the right-sentinel start), `α = []`
+(the buffer head starts just past the word; `rewindTM_run_ofCfg` allows `n = |word|`).

+Turing.exists_ndInterpreterCore : ∀ (c : Turing.EffectiveNDMachineCode),
+  ∃ K Q x x R j entry accept reject,
+    accept ≠ reject ∧
+      ∀ (α : List Bool),
+        ∃ C,
+          ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ) (s : List Bool),
+            t ≤ s.length →
+              have z := Turing.pairEncode (Turing.pairEncode t.bits α) x;
+              have N := (c.decode α).toFinNDTM;
+              have start :=
+                { state := some entry, inputPos := 1,
+                  workTapes := fun i => if i = j then Turing.FinTM.bufferTape s else fun x => none,
+                  workTapePos := fun x => 0, output := [] };
+              have e := Turing.NDTM.runWith (List.take t s) (Turing.NDTM.initCfg x);
+              ∃ T,
+                (∀ t' ≤ t,
+                    (Turing.NDTM.runWith (List.take t' s) (Turing.NDTM.initCfg x)).state = none →
+                      T ≤ C * (t' + x.length + t.bits.length + 1)) ∧
+                  T ≤ C * (t + 1) ∧
+                    (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom start T).state =
+                        some (if e.state = none ∧ e.output = [true] then accept else reject) ∧
+                      ∀ u < T,
+                        (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom start u).state ≠ some accept ∧
+                          (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom start u).state ≠ some reject

+                (c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t)) →

+      ∀ (α : List Bool),

+            ((c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t →
+                BF.ComputesInTime (Turing.pairEncode (Turing.pairEncode t.bits α) x) [true] (C * 2 ^ (C * (t + 1)))) ∧
+              (¬(c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t →
+                BF.ComputesInTime (Turing.pairEncode (Turing.pairEncode t.bits α) x) [false] (C * 2 ^ (C * (t + 1))))

+@Turing.expandTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {k : ℕ} {x : List Bool} (i : Fin k) (pat : List Bool)

+  c.state = some 0 →

+      (∀ (p : ℤ), -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → ct (cp + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →

+                  (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)

+                Turing.counterLoopCfg (some Turing.CounterLoopState.done)
+                  { state := c.state, inputPos := c.inputPos,
+                    workTapes := fun j q =>
+                      if j = i ∧ c.workTapePos j ≤ q ∧ q < c.workTapePos j + ↑(d * pat.length) then
+                        Turing.FinTM.bufferTape (List.replicate d pat).flatten (q - c.workTapePos j)

+                    workTapePos := Function.update c.workTapePos i (c.workTapePos i + ↑(d * pat.length)),
+                    output := c.output }
+                  (fun q => if cp ≤ q ∧ q < cp + ↑w.length then some true else ct q) cp ∧
+              (∀ t < d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1),
+                  (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                          (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)

+                  ∀ t ≤ d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1),
+                    ∀ (j : Fin k),

+                                (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct

+                                t).workTapePos

+                                (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct
+                                  cp)
+                                t).workTapePos
+                            j.castSucc =
+                          c.workTapePos j
+@Turing.clearTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (w : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SweepPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SweepPhase.sweep →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ), -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (2 * w.length + 2) =
+          { state := some Turing.SweepPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos,
+            workTapes := fun j q =>
+              if j = i ∧ d.workTapePos j ≤ q ∧ q < d.workTapePos j + ↑w.length then none else d.workTapes j q,
+            workTapePos := d.workTapePos, output := d.output } ∧
+        (∀ t < 2 * w.length + 2, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.SweepPhase.done) ∧
+          ∀ (j : Fin k),
+            ∀ t ≤ 2 * w.length + 2,
+              if j = i then
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                  Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j - 1) (d.workTapePos j + ↑w.length)
+              else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
+@Turing.skipBackTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (u : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SeekPhase.run →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ),
+        -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑u.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i - ↑u.length - 1 + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape u p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (u.length + 3) =
+          { state := some Turing.SeekPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos, workTapes := d.workTapes,
+            workTapePos := Function.update d.workTapePos i (d.workTapePos i - ↑u.length - 1), output := d.output } ∧
+        (∀ t < u.length + 3, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.SeekPhase.done) ∧
+          ∀ (j : Fin k),
+            ∀ t ≤ u.length + 3,
+              if j = i then
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                  Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j - ↑u.length - 2) (d.workTapePos j)
+              else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
+@Turing.seamCompTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S₁ : Type u_1} {S₂ : Type u_2} {Symbol : Type u_3} {x : List Symbol}
+  [inst : DecidableEq S₁] (M₁ : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S₁) (exit : S₁) (M₂ : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S₂)
+  (entry : S₂) {c₀ c₁ : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S₁ x} {c₃ : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S₂ x} {T₁ T₂ : ℕ},
+  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c₀ T₁ = c₁ →

+      (∀ t < T₁, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c₀ t).state ≠ some exit) →
+        Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => entry) c₁) T₂ = c₃ →
+          Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl c₀) (T₁ + 1 + T₂) = Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inr c₃
+@Turing.seamReleaseTM_firstReturn : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol}
+  (M : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S) (anchor : S) {c c' : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S x} {T : ℕ},

+    0 < T →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T = c' →
+        c'.state = some anchor →

+            Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => Sum.inl ()) c) T =
+                Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inr c' ∧

+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => Sum.inl ()) c) t).state ≠
+                  some (Sum.inr anchor)
+@Turing.phaseHostTM_run : ∀ {k : ℕ} {Γ I : Type} {Ph : I → Type} (M : (i : I) → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Γ (Ph i))
+  (q0 : (i : I) × Ph i) (red : (i : I) → Ph i → Option ((i : I) × Ph i)) (i : I) {x : List Γ}
+  (c c' : Turing.Cfg k Γ (Ph i) x) (T : ℕ),
+  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T = c' →
+    ∀ (s' : Ph i),
+      c'.state = some s' →

+                    Turing.Cfg.mapState (Sigma.mk i) (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c u)) ∧
+                Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (Sigma.mk i) c) (T + 1) =
+                  Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => q') c'

+Turing.FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapes_cast : ∀ (M₁ M₂ : Turing.FinTM Bool) {x y : List Bool}
+  (c : Turing.Cfg M₂.k Bool M₂.State y) (b : Bool) (p : Fin (x.length + 2)) (tapes : Fin M₁.k → ℤ → Option Bool)
+  (heads : Fin M₁.k → ℤ) (i : Fin (M₁.bufferedCompTM M₂).k),
+  (M₁.bufferedSecondCfg M₂ c b p tapes heads).workTapes i =
+    Turing.FinTM.tapeBlocks tapes (Turing.FinTM.bufferTape y) c.workTapes (Fin.cast ⋯ i)
+Turing.FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapePos_cast : ∀ (M₁ M₂ : Turing.FinTM Bool) {x y : List Bool}
+  (c : Turing.Cfg M₂.k Bool M₂.State y) (b : Bool) (p : Fin (x.length + 2)) (tapes : Fin M₁.k → ℤ → Option Bool)
+  (heads : Fin M₁.k → ℤ) (i : Fin (M₁.bufferedCompTM M₂).k),
+  (M₁.bufferedSecondCfg M₂ c b p tapes heads).workTapePos i =
+    Turing.FinTM.tapeBlocks heads (↑↑c.inputPos - 1) c.workTapePos (Fin.cast ⋯ i)

+@Turing.embedSilentRetTM : {m k : ℕ} →
+  {S : Type u_1} →
+    {Symbol : Type u_2} →
+      (Fin m ↪ Fin k) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM m Symbol S → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol (S ⊕ Unit)
+@Turing.embedSilentRetTM_run : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k),
+  ∀ cap ∉ Set.range ⇑ι,
+    ∀ (M : Turing.MultiTapeTM m Symbol S) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ)
+      (pre out₀ : List Symbol) (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x) (T : ℕ),
+      c.state ≠ none →
+        (∀ t < T, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c t).state ≠ none) →
+          (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T).state = none →
+            (∀ t < T,
+                Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                    (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) t =
+                  Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl
+                    (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c t))) ∧
+              (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                    (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) T =
+                  have __src := Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T);
+                  { state := some (Sum.inr ()), inputPos := __src.inputPos, workTapes := __src.workTapes,
+                    workTapePos := __src.workTapePos, output := __src.output }) ∧
+                ∀ t < T,
+                  (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                        (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) t).state ≠
+                    some (Sum.inr ())
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg : {m k : ℕ} →
+  {S : Type u_1} →
+    {Symbol : Type u_2} →
+      {x : List Symbol} →
+        (Fin m ↪ Fin k) →
+          Fin k →
+            (Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) →
+              (Fin k → ℤ) → List Symbol → List Symbol → Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x → Turing.Cfg k Symbol S x
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg_unselected : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k)
+  (cap : Fin k) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ) (pre out₀ : List Symbol)
+  (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x),
+  ∀ j ∉ Set.range ⇑ι,
+    j ≠ cap →
+      (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).workTapes j = tapes j ∧
+        (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).workTapePos j = heads j

+@Turing.MultiTapeTM.exists_first_halt : ∀ {k : ℕ} {Symbol : Type u_1} {State : Type u_2} {input : List Symbol}
+  (tm : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol State) (cfg : Turing.Cfg k Symbol State input) {t : ℕ},

+    ∃ u ≤ t,
+      (∀ v < u, ¬(Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg v).Halted) ∧
+        Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg u = Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg t
+@Turing.skipTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (w : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SeekPhase.run →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ), 0 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (w.length + 1) =
+          { state := some Turing.SeekPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos, workTapes := d.workTapes,
+            workTapePos := Function.update d.workTapePos i (d.workTapePos i + ↑w.length + 1), output := d.output } ∧

+@Turing.FinTM.rewind_heads : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type} {x : List Bool} (tm : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Bool S) (start scan : S)
+  (dest : Option S),
+  (∀ (inp : Option Bool) (work : Fin k → Option Bool),
+      tm.tr start inp work = Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.neg (some scan)) →
+    (∀ (inp : Option Bool) (work : Fin k → Option Bool),
+        tm.tr scan inp work =
+          match inp with
+          | some val => Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.neg (some scan)
+          | none => Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.pos dest) →
+      ∀ (c : Turing.Cfg k Bool S x),
+        c.state = some start →
+          ∃ r ≤ ↑c.inputPos + 2,
+            Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c r =
+                { state := dest, inputPos := 1, workTapes := c.workTapes, workTapePos := c.workTapePos,
+                  output := c.output } ∧
+              ∀ u ≤ r, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c u).workTapePos = c.workTapePos
+@Turing.FinTM.controlAction : {k : ℕ} → {S : Type} → SignType → Option S → Turing.Action k Bool S
+@Turing.FinTM.controlAction_apply : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type} {x : List Bool} (cfg : Turing.Cfg k Bool S x) (m : SignType)
+  (q : Option S),
+  (Turing.FinTM.controlAction m q).apply cfg =
+    { state := q, inputPos := Turing.moveInputPos cfg.inputPos m, workTapes := cfg.workTapes,
+      workTapePos := cfg.workTapePos, output := cfg.output }
+@Turing.FinTM.bufferTape_eq : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} (w : List Symbol) (z : ℤ),
+  Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w z = if 0 ≤ z then w[z.toNat]? else none

+  z < 0 → Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w z = none
+@Turing.FinTM.bufferTape_nat : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} (w : List Symbol) (i : ℕ), Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w ↑i = w[i]?
+Turing.length_pairEncode : ∀ (x y : List Bool), (Turing.pairEncode x y).length = 2 * x.length + 2 + y.length

++/-- **The universal NDTM from a setup machine.** Given, for the interpreter core's tape count
++`K` and stream tape `jR`, a deterministic setup `P` that on `⟨⟨bits t, α⟩, x⟩` first reaches its
++exit within `C_P·(t + 1)` steps with a block of exactly `t` nonblank cells on the core's stream
++tape from cell `0`, every other tape blank, every head at the origin, the input head at `1` and
++empty output, the guess-then-run assembly over the interpreter core is a universal NDTM at
++linear overhead.
++
++**Proof sketch.** Take the core `R` of `Turing.exists_ndInterpreterCore` and the setup `P`; the
++universal machine is `Turing.guessRunTM P pexit (ι jR) (embedSilentTM ι cap R) entry acc rej`.
++For each stream `s` of length `t`, the core reaches its verdict within `C_R·(t + 1)` from
++`streamStartCfg` (assembled from its field equations), so `Turing.guessRunTM_silent_spec` gives
++halting within `T_P + 2t + C_R·(t + 1) + 11 ≤ (C_P + C_R + 13)·(t + 1)` on every branch, and
++acceptance iff some guessed `s` is an accepting branch of length `t`
++(`Turing.FinNDTM.acceptsWithin_iff`). -/
++private theorem un_universal_of_setup (c : EffectiveNDMachineCode)
++    (hsetup : ∀ (K : ℕ) (jR : Fin K), ∃ (k : ℕ) (ι : Fin K ↪ Fin k) (cap : Fin k),
++      cap ∉ Set.range ι ∧ ∃ (SP : Type) (_ : Fintype SP) (_ : DecidableEq SP)
++        (P : MultiTapeTM k Bool SP) (pexit : SP),
++        ∀ α : List Bool, ∃ CP : ℕ, ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ),
++          ∃ TP ≤ CP * (t + 1), ∃ blk : ℤ → Option Bool,
++            (∀ p : ℤ, blk p ≠ none ↔ 0 ≤ p ∧ p < t) ∧
++            P.runFrom (P.initCfg (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x)) TP =
++              ⟨some pexit, 1, Function.update (fun _ _ => none) (ι jR) blk, fun _ => 0, []⟩ ∧
++            ∀ u < TP,
++              (P.runFrom (P.initCfg (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x)) u).state ≠
++                some pexit) :
++    ∃ UN : FinNDTM Bool, ∀ α : List Bool, ∃ C : ℕ, ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ),
++      UN.tm.HaltsWithin (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) (C * (t + 1)) ∧
++      (UN.AcceptsWithin (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x) (C * (t + 1)) ↔
++        (c.decode α).toFinNDTM.AcceptsWithin x t) := by

++  obtain ⟨CR, hCR⟩ := hR α

++  refine ⟨CP + CR + 13, fun x t => ?_⟩

++/-! ### The setup machine's pieces (continuation UN cont1)
++
++`un_universal_of_setup` above reduces target 1 to its setup hypothesis. The setup built below
++is a seam chain over `K + kC + 1` tapes — the core's `K`, the embedded header composite's `kC`,
++and the capture/counter tape last — whose phases are the embedded header pass, the code buffer's
++rewind and clear, a guided erase of the extractor scratch bank, the expansion of the captured
++budget word into the unary block, the block rewind, the counter clear, and the input rewind.
++The pieces here are the two leaf machines with their framed runs, the seam-step composer, the
++header composite's exact first halt, and two small word facts. -/
++
++/-- Flattening `t` copies of the one-symbol pattern gives the length-`t` unary block. -/
++private lemma un_flatten (t : ℕ) :
++    (List.replicate t [true]).flatten = List.replicate t true := by
++  induction t with
++  | zero => rfl
++  | succ t ih => simp [List.replicate_succ, ih]
++
++/-- A buffered word is blank at and beyond its length. -/
++private lemma un_buffer_ge (w : List Bool) (q : ℤ) (hq : (w.length : ℤ) ≤ q) :
++    FinTM.bufferTape w q = none := by
++  have h0 : 0 ≤ q := le_trans (Int.natCast_nonneg _) hq
++  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n : ℕ, q = (n : ℤ) := ⟨q.toNat, (Int.toNat_of_nonneg h0).symm⟩
++  rw [FinTM.bufferTape_nat]
++  exact List.getElem?_eq_none (by exact_mod_cast hq)
++
++/-- One seam step: a chain that runs to its exit anchor with a first-visit cut, composed with
++a next phase that runs from the handover to its own anchor with its own cut, yields the
++composite's run, its anchor state and its inherited cut in one package. -/
++private theorem un_step {k : ℕ} {S₁ S₂ : Type} [DecidableEq S₁] {z : List Bool}
++    (M₁ : MultiTapeTM k Bool S₁) (exit : S₁) (M₂ : MultiTapeTM k Bool S₂) (entry : S₂)
++    (q₂ : S₂) {c₀ c₁ : Cfg k Bool S₁ z} {c₃ : Cfg k Bool S₂ z} {T₁ T₂ : ℕ}
++    (h₁ : M₁.runFrom c₀ T₁ = c₁) (hexit : c₁.state = some exit)
++    (hcut : ∀ t < T₁, (M₁.runFrom c₀ t).state ≠ some exit)
++    (h₂ : M₂.runFrom (c₁.mapState fun _ => entry) T₂ = c₃)
++    (hq : c₃.state = some q₂)
++    (hcut₂ : ∀ t < T₂, (M₂.runFrom (c₁.mapState fun _ => entry) t).state ≠ some q₂) :
++    (seamCompTM M₁ exit M₂ entry).runFrom (c₀.mapState Sum.inl) (T₁ + 1 + T₂) =
++        c₃.mapState Sum.inr ∧
++      (c₃.mapState (Sum.inr : S₂ → S₁ ⊕ S₂)).state = some (Sum.inr q₂) ∧
++      ∀ t < T₁ + 1 + T₂,
++        ((seamCompTM M₁ exit M₂ entry).runFrom (c₀.mapState Sum.inl) t).state ≠
++          some (Sum.inr q₂) :=
++  ⟨seamCompTM_run_ofCfg M₁ exit M₂ entry h₁ hexit hcut h₂,
++    by rw [Cfg.mapState_state, hq, Option.map_some],
++    seamCompTM_firstReturn_ofCfg M₁ exit M₂ entry q₂ h₁ hexit hcut h₂ hq hcut₂⟩
++
++/-- **The header composite's exact first halt**, uniformly in the input suffix: the buffered
++composite of the prefix parser and the first-component extractor, on
++`pairEncode (pairEncode a b) x`, halts at exactly `3·|pairEncode a b| + 4 + (4·|a| + 5)` and is
++live before. -/
++private theorem un_canon_halt (a b x : List Bool) :
++    ((FinTM.bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false)).tm.runFrom
++        ((FinTM.bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false)).tm.initCfg
++          (pairEncode (pairEncode a b) x))
++        (3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4 + (4 * a.length + 5))).state = none ∧
++      ∀ v < 3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4 + (4 * a.length + 5),
++        ((FinTM.bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false)).tm.runFrom
++          ((FinTM.bufferedCompTM universalPrefixTM (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false)).tm.initCfg
++            (pairEncode (pairEncode a b) x)) v).state ≠ none := by
++  obtain ⟨hrun, hlive⟩ := FinTM.pairExtractTM_fst_run a b
++  constructor
++  · obtain ⟨b', hb⟩ := prefixCanon_run (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false) (pairEncode a b) x
++      (4 * a.length + 5)
++    simp only [prefixCanonTM_eq] at hb
++    rw [hb, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_state, hrun]
++    rfl
++  · intro v hv
++    by_cases hpre : v < 3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4
++    · have hs := prefixCanon_start (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false) (pairEncode a b) x
++      simp only [prefixCanonTM_eq] at hs
++      refine MultiTapeTM.runFrom_state_ne_none_of_le _ _
++        (3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4) ?_ v (by omega)
++      rw [hs, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_state, MultiTapeTM.initCfg_eq, Cfg.init_state,
++        Option.map_some]
++      exact Option.some_ne_none _
++    · obtain ⟨b', hb⟩ := prefixCanon_run (FinTM.pairExtractTM true false) (pairEncode a b) x
++        (v - (3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4))
++      simp only [prefixCanonTM_eq] at hb
++      rw [Nat.add_sub_of_le (by omega : 3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4 ≤ v)] at hb
++      rw [hb, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_state]
++      intro h
++      exact hlive (v - (3 * (pairEncode a b).length + 4)) (by omega)
++        (Option.map_eq_none_iff.mp h)
++
++/-- The bank erase's one action: blank the selected tapes and move them with the guide. -/
++private def un_bankAct (k : ℕ) (S : Finset (Fin k)) (g : Fin k) (m : SignType) (q' : Fin 3) :
++    Action k Bool (Fin 3) :=
++  ⟨0, fun i => if i ∈ S then (some none, m) else if i = g then (none, m) else (none, 0),
++    none, some q'⟩
++
++private lemma un_bankAct_wr {k : ℕ} (S : Finset (Fin k)) (g : Fin k) (m : SignType)
++    (q' : Fin 3) (i : Fin k) :
++    ((un_bankAct k S g m q').workTapes i).1 = if i ∈ S then some none else none := by
++  by_cases hiS : i ∈ S
++  · simp [un_bankAct, hiS]
++  · by_cases hig : i = g
++    · subst hig; simp [un_bankAct, hiS]
++    · simp [un_bankAct, hiS, hig]
++
++private lemma un_bankAct_mv {k : ℕ} (S : Finset (Fin k)) (g : Fin k) (m : SignType)
++    (q' : Fin 3) (i : Fin k) :
++    (((un_bankAct k S g m q').workTapes i).2 : ℤ) =
++      if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (m : ℤ) else 0 := by
++  by_cases hiS : i ∈ S
++  · simp [un_bankAct, hiS]
++  · by_cases hig : i = g
++    · subst hig; simp [un_bankAct, hiS]
++    · simp [un_bankAct, hiS, hig]
++
++/-- **The guided bank erase**: one unconditional left step, a left sweep that blanks every
++selected tape while the guide tape still shows a symbol under the travelling head, and one
++step right into the live park. The guide's head travels with the bank but its word is kept;
++every other tape, every other head, the input head and the output are untouched. -/
++private def un_bankTM (k : ℕ) (S : Finset (Fin k)) (g : Fin k) :
++    MultiTapeTM k Bool (Fin 3) where
++  q₀ := 0
++  tr := fun q _ w =>
++    if q = 0 then un_bankAct k S g .neg 1
++    else if q = 1 then
++      match w g with
++      | some _ => un_bankAct k S g .neg 1
++      | none => un_bankAct k S g .pos 2
++    else ⟨0, fun _ => (none, 0), none, some 2⟩
++
++/-- **The guided bank erase, framed.** Start with every bank tape and the guide holding a
++length-`L` buffered word with its head at `L`. The erase parks at exactly `L + 2` and not
++earlier, with the bank blank, the bank and guide heads at the origin, the guide's word and
++everything else unchanged. -/
++private theorem un_bank_run {k : ℕ} {z : List Bool} (S : Finset (Fin k)) (g : Fin k)
++    (hg : g ∉ S) (L : ℕ) (w : Fin k → List Bool) (hw : ∀ i, i ∈ S ∨ i = g → (w i).length = L)
++    (d : Cfg k Bool (Fin 3) z) (hstate : d.state = some 0)
++    (htape : ∀ i, i ∈ S ∨ i = g → d.workTapes i = FinTM.bufferTape (w i))
++    (hpos : ∀ i, i ∈ S ∨ i = g → d.workTapePos i = (L : ℤ)) :
++    (un_bankTM k S g).runFrom d (L + 2) =
++        { d with
++          state := some 2
++          workTapes := fun i => if i ∈ S then (fun _ => none) else d.workTapes i
++          workTapePos := fun i => if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then 0 else d.workTapePos i } ∧
++      ∀ t < L + 2, ((un_bankTM k S g).runFrom d t).state ≠ some 2 := by
++  classical
++  -- the sweep's trajectory
++  let T : ℕ → Cfg k Bool (Fin 3) z := fun r =>
++    { d with
++      state := some (if r = 0 then 0 else 1)
++      workTapes := fun i =>
++        if i ∈ S then (fun q => if (L : ℤ) - r < q then none else FinTM.bufferTape (w i) q)
++        else d.workTapes i
++      workTapePos := fun i => if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - r else d.workTapePos i }
++  have hT0 : T 0 = d := by
++    refine Cfg.ext ?_ rfl ?_ ?_ rfl
++    · show some (if 0 = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1) = d.state
++      rw [if_pos rfl, hstate]
++    · funext i q
++      show (if i ∈ S then (fun q => if (L : ℤ) - (0 : ℕ) < q then none
++          else FinTM.bufferTape (w i) q) else d.workTapes i) q = d.workTapes i q
++      by_cases hiS : i ∈ S
++      · rw [if_pos hiS, htape i (Or.inl hiS)]
++        by_cases hq : (L : ℤ) - (0 : ℕ) < q
++        · rw [if_pos hq, eq_comm]
++          exact un_buffer_ge _ _ (by rw [hw i (Or.inl hiS)]; push_cast at hq ⊢; omega)
++        · rw [if_neg hq]
++      · rw [if_neg hiS]
++    · funext i
++      show (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - (0 : ℕ) else d.workTapePos i) = d.workTapePos i
++      by_cases hig : i ∈ S ∨ i = g
++      · rw [if_pos hig, hpos i hig]
++        omega
++      · rw [if_neg hig]
++  -- one erasing step of the sweep
++  have happly : ∀ r : ℕ, r ≤ L →
++      (un_bankAct k S g .neg 1).apply (T r) = T (r + 1) := by
++    intro r hr
++    refine Cfg.ext ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
++    · rw [Action.apply_state]
++      show some 1 = some (if r + 1 = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1)
++      rw [if_neg (Nat.succ_ne_zero r)]
++    · rw [Action.apply_inputPos]
++      show moveInputPos (T r).inputPos (un_bankAct k S g .neg 1).inputTape = (T (r + 1)).inputPos
++      rw [show (un_bankAct k S g .neg 1).inputTape = 0 from rfl, moveInputPos_zero]
++    · funext i
++      rw [Action.apply_workTapes, un_bankAct_wr]
++      by_cases hiS : i ∈ S
++      · rw [if_pos hiS, Option.getD_some]
++        funext q
++        rw [Function.update_apply]
++        have hps : (T r).workTapePos i = (L : ℤ) - r := by
++          show (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - r else d.workTapePos i) = _
++          rw [if_pos (Or.inl hiS)]
++        have htp : (T r).workTapes i =
++            fun q => if (L : ℤ) - r < q then none else FinTM.bufferTape (w i) q := by
++          show (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i) = _
++          rw [if_pos hiS]
++        have htp' : (T (r + 1)).workTapes i =
++            fun q => if (L : ℤ) - (r + 1 : ℕ) < q then none else FinTM.bufferTape (w i) q := by
++          show (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i) = _
++          rw [if_pos hiS]
++        rw [hps, htp, htp']
++        dsimp only
++        by_cases hq : q = (L : ℤ) - r
++        · rw [if_pos hq, eq_comm,
++            if_pos (show (L : ℤ) - (r + 1 : ℕ) < q by push_cast; omega)]
++        · rw [if_neg hq]
++          by_cases hql : (L : ℤ) - r < q
++          · rw [if_pos hql, if_pos (show (L : ℤ) - (r + 1 : ℕ) < q by push_cast at hql ⊢; omega)]
++          · rw [if_neg hql, if_neg (show ¬((L : ℤ) - (r + 1 : ℕ) < q) by
++              push_cast at hq hql ⊢; omega)]
++      · rw [if_neg hiS, Cfg.workTapeSymbols_eq, Option.getD_none, Function.update_eq_self]
++        show (T r).workTapes i = (T (r + 1)).workTapes i
++        show (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i) = (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i)
++        rw [if_neg hiS, if_neg hiS]
++    · funext i
++      rw [Action.apply_workTapePos]
++      have hmv := un_bankAct_mv S g .neg 1 i
++      show (T r).workTapePos i + (((un_bankAct k S g .neg 1).workTapes i).2 : ℤ) =
++        (T (r + 1)).workTapePos i
++      rw [hmv]
++      show (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - r else d.workTapePos i) + _ =
++        (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - (r + 1 : ℕ) else d.workTapePos i)
++      by_cases hig : i ∈ S ∨ i = g
++      · rw [if_pos hig, if_pos hig, if_pos hig]
++        rw [SignType.neg_eq_neg_one, SignType.coe_neg_one]
++        omega
++      · rw [if_neg hig, if_neg hig, if_neg hig, add_zero]
++    · rw [Action.apply_output]
++      show (T r).output ++ (none : Option Bool).toList = (T (r + 1)).output
++      rw [Option.toList_none, List.append_nil]
++  have hstep : ∀ r : ℕ, r ≤ L → (un_bankTM k S g).step (T r) = T (r + 1) := by
++    intro r hr
++    have hs : (T r).state = some (if r = 0 then 0 else 1) := rfl
++    rw [MultiTapeTM.step_of_some hs]
++    have htr : (un_bankTM k S g).tr (if r = 0 then 0 else 1) (T r).inputSymbol
++        (T r).workTapeSymbols = un_bankAct k S g .neg 1 := by
++      by_cases hr0 : r = 0
++      · rw [if_pos hr0]
++        simp only [un_bankTM, Fin.reduceEq, ↓reduceIte]
++      · rw [if_neg hr0]
++        have hread : (T r).workTapeSymbols g =
++            FinTM.bufferTape (w g) ((L : ℤ) - r) := by
++          rw [Cfg.workTapeSymbols_eq]
++          have h1 : (T r).workTapes g = FinTM.bufferTape (w g) := by
++            show (if g ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes g) = _
++            rw [if_neg hg, htape g (Or.inr rfl)]
++          have h2 : (T r).workTapePos g = (L : ℤ) - r := by
++            show (if g ∈ S ∨ g = g then (L : ℤ) - r else d.workTapePos g) = _
++            rw [if_pos (Or.inr rfl)]
++          rw [h1, h2]
++        have hsome : ∃ b, FinTM.bufferTape (w g) ((L : ℤ) - r) = some b := by
++          have hlt : L - r < (w g).length := by rw [hw g (Or.inr rfl)]; omega
++          refine ⟨(w g)[L - r], ?_⟩
++          rw [show (L : ℤ) - r = ((L - r : ℕ) : ℤ) by push_cast; omega,
++            FinTM.bufferTape_nat, List.getElem?_eq_getElem hlt]
++        obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := hsome
++        simp only [un_bankTM, Fin.reduceEq, ↓reduceIte]
++        rw [hread, hb]
++    rw [htr]
++    exact happly r hr
++  have hrun : ∀ r : ℕ, r ≤ L + 1 → (un_bankTM k S g).runFrom d r = T r := by
++    intro r hr
++    induction r with
++    | zero => rw [MultiTapeTM.runFrom_zero, hT0]
++    | succ r ih =>
++      rw [MultiTapeTM.runFrom_succ_eq_step', ih (by omega), hstep r (by omega)]
++  -- the final turning step
++  have hfinal : (un_bankTM k S g).step (T (L + 1)) =
++      { d with
++        state := some 2
++        workTapes := fun i => if i ∈ S then (fun _ => none) else d.workTapes i
++        workTapePos := fun i => if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then 0 else d.workTapePos i } := by
++    have hs : (T (L + 1)).state = some 1 := by
++      show some (if L + 1 = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1) = some 1
++      rw [if_neg (Nat.succ_ne_zero L)]
++    rw [MultiTapeTM.step_of_some hs]
++    have hread : (T (L + 1)).workTapeSymbols g = none := by
++      rw [Cfg.workTapeSymbols_eq]
++      have h1 : (T (L + 1)).workTapes g = FinTM.bufferTape (w g) := by
++        show (if g ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes g) = _
++        rw [if_neg hg, htape g (Or.inr rfl)]
++      have h2 : (T (L + 1)).workTapePos g = (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) := by
++        show (if g ∈ S ∨ g = g then (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) else d.workTapePos g) = _
++        rw [if_pos (Or.inr rfl)]
++      rw [h1, h2]
++      exact FinTM.bufferTape_of_neg _ (by push_cast; omega)
++    have htr : (un_bankTM k S g).tr 1 (T (L + 1)).inputSymbol (T (L + 1)).workTapeSymbols =
++        un_bankAct k S g .pos 2 := by
++      simp only [un_bankTM, Fin.reduceEq, ↓reduceIte]
++      rw [hread]
++    rw [htr]
++    refine Cfg.ext ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
++    · rw [Action.apply_state]
++      rfl
++    · rw [Action.apply_inputPos]
++      rw [show (un_bankAct k S g .pos 2).inputTape = 0 from rfl, moveInputPos_zero]
++    · funext i
++      rw [Action.apply_workTapes, un_bankAct_wr]
++      by_cases hiS : i ∈ S
++      · rw [if_pos hiS, Option.getD_some]
++        show Function.update ((T (L + 1)).workTapes i) ((T (L + 1)).workTapePos i) none =
++          (if i ∈ S then (fun _ => none) else d.workTapes i)
++        rw [if_pos hiS]
++        have htp : (T (L + 1)).workTapes i =
++            fun q => if (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) < q then none else FinTM.bufferTape (w i) q := by
++          show (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i) = _
++          rw [if_pos hiS]
++        have hps : (T (L + 1)).workTapePos i = (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) := by
++          show (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) else d.workTapePos i) = _
++          rw [if_pos (Or.inl hiS)]
++        rw [htp, hps]
++        funext q
++        rw [Function.update_apply]
++        try dsimp only
++        by_cases hq : q = (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ)
++        · rw [if_pos hq]
++        · rw [if_neg hq]
++          by_cases hql : (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) < q
++          · rw [if_pos hql]
++          · rw [if_neg hql]
++            exact FinTM.bufferTape_of_neg _ (by push_cast at hql ⊢; omega)
++      · rw [if_neg hiS, Cfg.workTapeSymbols_eq, Option.getD_none, Function.update_eq_self]
++        show (T (L + 1)).workTapes i = (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i)
++        show (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i) = (if i ∈ S then _ else d.workTapes i)
++        rw [if_neg hiS, if_neg hiS]
++    · funext i
++      rw [Action.apply_workTapePos]
++      have hmv := un_bankAct_mv S g .pos 2 i
++      show (T (L + 1)).workTapePos i + (((un_bankAct k S g .pos 2).workTapes i).2 : ℤ) =
++        (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then 0 else d.workTapePos i)
++      rw [hmv]
++      show (if i ∈ S ∨ i = g then (L : ℤ) - (L + 1 : ℕ) else d.workTapePos i) + _ = _
++      by_cases hig : i ∈ S ∨ i = g
++      · rw [if_pos hig, if_pos hig, if_pos hig]
++        rw [SignType.pos_eq_one, SignType.coe_one]
++        omega
++      · rw [if_neg hig, if_neg hig, if_neg hig, add_zero]
++    · rw [Action.apply_output]
++      show (T (L + 1)).output ++ (none : Option Bool).toList = d.output
++      rw [Option.toList_none, List.append_nil]
++  constructor
++  · rw [show L + 2 = (L + 1) + 1 by omega, MultiTapeTM.runFrom_succ_eq_step',
++      hrun (L + 1) le_rfl, hfinal]
++  · intro t ht
++    rw [hrun t (by omega)]
++    show some (if t = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1) ≠ some 2
++    by_cases ht0 : t = 0
++    · rw [if_pos ht0]; decide
++    · rw [if_neg ht0]; decide
++
++/-- **The input rewind leaf** (`Turing.FinTM.controlAction`): one unconditional left step, a
++left scan while the input head reads a symbol, one step right into the live park. No work
++tape, work head or output is touched. -/
++private def un_inRewTM (k : ℕ) : MultiTapeTM k Bool (Fin 3) where
++  q₀ := 0
++  tr := fun q inp _ =>
++    if q = 0 then FinTM.controlAction .neg (some 1)
++    else if q = 1 then
++      match inp with
++      | some _ => FinTM.controlAction .neg (some 1)
++      | none => FinTM.controlAction .pos (some 2)
++    else FinTM.controlAction 0 (some 2)
++
++/-- **The input rewind leaf, framed**: from a start whose input head is at `p ≥ 1`, it parks at
++exactly `p + 1` and not earlier, with the input head at `1` and every other field unchanged. -/
++private theorem un_inRew_run {k : ℕ} {z : List Bool} (d : Cfg k Bool (Fin 3) z)
++    (hstate : d.state = some 0) (hp : 1 ≤ d.inputPos.val) :
++    (un_inRewTM k).runFrom d (d.inputPos.val + 1) =
++        { d with state := some 2, inputPos := 1 } ∧
++      ∀ t < d.inputPos.val + 1, ((un_inRewTM k).runFrom d t).state ≠ some 2 := by
++  classical
++  have hlt := d.inputPos.isLt
++  -- the scan's trajectory
++  let T : ℕ → Cfg k Bool (Fin 3) z := fun r =>
++    { d with
++      state := some (if r = 0 then 0 else 1)
++      inputPos := ⟨d.inputPos.val - r, by omega⟩ }
++  have hT0 : T 0 = d := by
++    refine Cfg.ext ?_ ?_ rfl rfl rfl
++    · show some (if 0 = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1) = d.state
++      rw [if_pos rfl, hstate]
++    · refine Fin.ext ?_
++      show d.inputPos.val - 0 = d.inputPos.val
++      omega
++  have hstep : ∀ r : ℕ, r ≤ d.inputPos.val - 1 →
++      (un_inRewTM k).step (T r) = T (r + 1) := by
++    intro r hr
++    have hs : (T r).state = some (if r = 0 then 0 else 1) := rfl
++    rw [MultiTapeTM.step_of_some hs]
++    have htr : (un_inRewTM k).tr (if r = 0 then 0 else 1) (T r).inputSymbol
++        (T r).workTapeSymbols = FinTM.controlAction .neg (some 1) := by
++      by_cases hr0 : r = 0
++      · rw [if_pos hr0]
++        simp only [un_inRewTM, Fin.reduceEq, ↓reduceIte]
++      · rw [if_neg hr0]
++        have hread : (T r).inputSymbol = some (z[d.inputPos.val - r - 1]'(by omega)) := by
++          refine inputSymbolInner (d.inputPos.val - r - 1) ?_ (by omega)
++          show d.inputPos.val - r = 1 + (d.inputPos.val - r - 1)
++          omega
++        rw [hread]
++        simp only [un_inRewTM, Fin.reduceEq, ↓reduceIte]
++    rw [htr, FinTM.controlAction_apply]
++    refine Cfg.ext ?_ ?_ rfl rfl rfl
++    · show some 1 = some (if r + 1 = 0 then (0 : Fin 3) else 1)
++      rw [if_neg (Nat.succ_ne_zero r)]
++    · show moveInputPos (T r).inputPos .neg = (T (r + 1)).inputPos

++/-- Collapsing a constant state relabeling over any previous relabeling. -/
++private lemma un_mapState_comp {k : ℕ} {z : List Bool} {S₁ S₂ S₃ : Type}
++    (c : Cfg k Bool S₁ z) (f : S₁ → S₂) (e : S₃) :
++    (c.mapState f).mapState (fun _ => e) = c.mapState (fun _ => e) := by
++  refine Cfg.ext ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_

++    cases c.state <;> rfl

++  · funext i q
++    rw [Cfg.mapState_workTapes, Cfg.mapState_workTapes, Cfg.mapState_workTapes]
++  · funext i
++    rw [Cfg.mapState_workTapePos, Cfg.mapState_workTapePos, Cfg.mapState_workTapePos]
++  · rw [Cfg.mapState_output, Cfg.mapState_output, Cfg.mapState_output]

++/-- One seam step whose previous endpoint arrives as a right-tagged configuration: the
++handover's constant relabeling collapses the tag, so the next phase's framed run applies
++verbatim. -/
++private theorem un_step' {k : ℕ} {S₀ S₁ S₂ : Type} [DecidableEq S₁] {z : List Bool}
++    (M₁ : MultiTapeTM k Bool S₁) (exit : S₁) (M₂ : MultiTapeTM k Bool S₂) (entry : S₂)
++    (q₂ : S₂) (g : S₀ → S₁) {c₀ : Cfg k Bool S₁ z} {c₂ : Cfg k Bool S₀ z}
++    {c₃ : Cfg k Bool S₂ z} {T₁ T₂ : ℕ}
++    (h₁ : M₁.runFrom c₀ T₁ = c₂.mapState g) (hexit : (c₂.mapState g).state = some exit)
++    (hcut : ∀ t < T₁, (M₁.runFrom c₀ t).state ≠ some exit)
++    (h₂ : M₂.runFrom (c₂.mapState fun _ => entry) T₂ = c₃)
++    (hq : c₃.state = some q₂)
++    (hcut₂ : ∀ t < T₂, (M₂.runFrom (c₂.mapState fun _ => entry) t).state ≠ some q₂) :
++    (seamCompTM M₁ exit M₂ entry).runFrom (c₀.mapState Sum.inl) (T₁ + 1 + T₂) =
++        c₃.mapState Sum.inr ∧
++      (c₃.mapState (Sum.inr : S₂ → S₁ ⊕ S₂)).state = some (Sum.inr q₂) ∧
++      ∀ t < T₁ + 1 + T₂,
++        ((seamCompTM M₁ exit M₂ entry).runFrom (c₀.mapState Sum.inl) t).state ≠
++          some (Sum.inr q₂) := by
++  have hcomp := un_mapState_comp c₂ g entry
++  exact un_step M₁ exit M₂ entry q₂ h₁ hexit hcut (by rw [hcomp]; exact h₂) hq
++    (fun t ht => by rw [hcomp] at *; exact hcut₂ t ht)
++
++/-- **The setup machine.** For the interpreter core's tape count `K` and stream tape `jR`,
++the seam chain over `K + kC + 1` tapes — the embedded header pass, the code buffer's rewind
++and clear, the guided bank erase, the expansion, the block rewind, the counter clear, and the
++input rewind — first reaches its park at the setup hypothesis's exit configuration, within a
++per-code linear budget.
++
++**Proof sketch.** The header composite halts at `3|u| + 4 + (4|bits t| + 5)` with the code
++buffer `u`, the extractor scratch `bits t` and the captured budget word `bits t` on `cap`
++(`Turing.prefixCanon_run`, `Turing.FinTM.pairExtractTM_fst_run`, the cast pair and the
++tape-block projections, through `Turing.embedSilentRetTM_run`). The buffer rewind and clear
++blank the code buffer; the guided bank erase blanks the extractor scratch while rewinding the
++capture head; the expansion writes the `t`-cell block on `ι jR` (`Turing.expandTM_run_ofCfg`,
++`Turing.counterValue_bits`); the block rewind, the counter clear and the input rewind reach
++the exit configuration. Each seam inherits the first-visit cut, and the ledger is linear by
++`Turing.counterOverhead_le` and `Turing.length_bits_le_self`. -/
++private theorem un_setup : ∀ (K : ℕ) (jR : Fin K), ∃ (k : ℕ) (ι : Fin K ↪ Fin k) (cap : Fin k),
++    cap ∉ Set.range ι ∧ ∃ (SP : Type) (_ : Fintype SP) (_ : DecidableEq SP)
++      (P : MultiTapeTM k Bool SP) (pexit : SP),
++      ∀ α : List Bool, ∃ CP : ℕ, ∀ (x : List Bool) (t : ℕ),
++        ∃ TP ≤ CP * (t + 1), ∃ blk : ℤ → Option Bool,
++          (∀ p : ℤ, blk p ≠ none ↔ 0 ≤ p ∧ p < t) ∧
++          P.runFrom (P.initCfg (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x)) TP =
++            ⟨some pexit, 1, Function.update (fun _ _ => none) (ι jR) blk, fun _ => 0, []⟩ ∧
++          ∀ u < TP,
++            (P.runFrom (P.initCfg (pairEncode (pairEncode (Nat.bits t) α) x)) u).state ≠
++              some pexit := by

++      embedSilentCfg_output ιC cap _ _ [] [] CC
++    have hcBuf : Fin.cast (FinTM.bufferedCompTM_k universalPrefixTM F) jBuf =
++        Fin.natAdd universalPrefixTM.k (Fin.castAdd F.k 0) := by
++      refine Fin.ext ?_
++      rw [Fin.coe_cast, hvjBuf]
++      simp [Fin.coe_natAdd, Fin.coe_castAdd, hup]
++    have hcEx : ∀ e : Fin F.k, Fin.cast (FinTM.bufferedCompTM_k universalPrefixTM F) (jEx e) =
++        Fin.natAdd universalPrefixTM.k (Fin.natAdd 1 e) := by
++      intro e
++      refine Fin.ext ?_
++      rw [Fin.coe_cast, hvjEx]
++      simp [Fin.coe_natAdd, hup]
++    have hnr : ∀ i : Fin (K + MC.k + 1), i ≠ iBuf → i ∉ Sx → i ∉ Set.range ιC := by
++      intro i hib hiS
++      rintro ⟨j, hj⟩
++      have hv := congrArg Fin.val hj
++      rw [hvιC] at hv
++      have hjlt : j.val < 1 + F.k := by
++        have h := j.isLt
++        omega
++      by_cases hj0 : j.val = 0
++      · exact hib (by rw [← hj, show j = jBuf from Fin.ext (by rw [hvjBuf]; exact hj0)])
++      · refine hiS ((hSx i).mpr ⟨⟨j.val - 1, by omega⟩, Fin.ext ?_⟩)
++        rw [hviEx]
++        show K + 1 + (j.val - 1) = i.val
++        omega
++    have htap : ∀ i, (embedSilentCfg ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC).workTapes i
++        = tp1 i := by
++      intro i
++      by_cases hib : i = iBuf
++      · rw [hib]
++        have h1 := embedSilentCfg_selected_tape ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC jBuf
++        have h2 : CC.workTapes jBuf = FinTM.bufferTape u := by
++          rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapes_cast, hcBuf, FinTM.tapeBlocks_buffer]
++        refine h1.trans (h2.trans ?_)
++        show _ = tp1 iBuf
++        show _ = (if iBuf = iBuf then FinTM.bufferTape u else if iBuf = cap then
++          FinTM.bufferTape bt else if iBuf ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt else fun _ => none)
++        rw [if_pos rfl]
++      · by_cases hic : i = cap
++        · rw [hic]
++          have h1 := (embedSilentCfg_cap ιC cap hcaprC (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC).1
++          have h2 : CC.output = bt := by
++            rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_output]
++          refine h1.trans ?_
++          rw [h2, List.nil_append]
++          show _ = (if cap = iBuf then FinTM.bufferTape u else if cap = cap then
++            FinTM.bufferTape bt else if cap ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt else fun _ => none)
++          rw [if_neg (Ne.symm hBufcap), if_pos rfl]
++        · by_cases hiS : i ∈ Sx
++          · obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := (hSx i).mp hiS
++            rw [← he]
++            have h1 := embedSilentCfg_selected_tape ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] []
++              CC (jEx e)
++            have h2 : CC.workTapes (jEx e) = FinTM.bufferTape bt := by
++              rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapes_cast, hcEx, FinTM.tapeBlocks_right]
++            refine h1.trans (h2.trans ?_)
++            have heS : iEx e ∈ Sx := (hSx (iEx e)).mpr ⟨e, rfl⟩
++            have h3 : iEx e ≠ iBuf := by
++              intro h
++              have hv := congrArg Fin.val h
++              rw [hviEx, hviBuf] at hv
++              omega
++            have h4 : iEx e ≠ cap := by
++              intro h
++              have hv := congrArg Fin.val h
++              rw [hviEx, hvcap] at hv
++              have := e.isLt
++              rw [hkC, hup] at hv
++              omega
++            show _ = (if iEx e = iBuf then FinTM.bufferTape u else if iEx e = cap then
++              FinTM.bufferTape bt else if iEx e ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt
++              else fun _ => none)
++            rw [if_neg h3, if_neg h4, if_pos heS]
++          · have h1 := (embedSilentCfg_unselected ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] []
++              CC i (hnr i hib hiS) hic).1
++            refine h1.trans ?_
++            show _ = (if i = iBuf then FinTM.bufferTape u else if i = cap then
++              FinTM.bufferTape bt else if i ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt else fun _ => none)
++            rw [if_neg hib, if_neg hic, if_neg hiS]
++    have hhd : ∀ i, (embedSilentCfg ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC).workTapePos i
++        = hd1 i := by
++      intro i
++      by_cases hib : i = iBuf
++      · rw [hib]
++        have h1 := embedSilentCfg_selected_pos ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC jBuf
++        have h2 : CC.workTapePos jBuf = (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℤ) := by
++          rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapePos_cast, hcBuf, FinTM.tapeBlocks_buffer]
++          push_cast
++          ring
++        refine h1.trans (h2.trans ?_)
++        show _ = (if iBuf = iBuf then (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℤ) else if iBuf = cap then
++          (bt.length : ℤ) else if iBuf ∈ Sx then (bt.length : ℤ) else 0)
++        rw [if_pos rfl]

++          have h1 := (embedSilentCfg_cap ιC cap hcaprC (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] [] CC).2

++            rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_output]

++          rw [h2, List.nil_append]

++            (bt.length : ℤ) else if cap ∈ Sx then (bt.length : ℤ) else 0)
++          rw [if_neg (Ne.symm hBufcap), if_pos rfl]

++          · obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := (hSx i).mp hiS
++            rw [← he]
++            have h1 := embedSilentCfg_selected_pos ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] []
++              CC (jEx e)
++            have h2 : CC.workTapePos (jEx e) = (bt.length : ℤ) := by
++              rw [hbM, FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapePos_cast, hcEx, FinTM.tapeBlocks_right]
++            refine h1.trans (h2.trans ?_)
++            have heS : iEx e ∈ Sx := (hSx (iEx e)).mpr ⟨e, rfl⟩
++            have h3 : iEx e ≠ iBuf := by
++              intro h
++              have hv := congrArg Fin.val h
++              rw [hviEx, hviBuf] at hv
++              omega
++            have h4 : iEx e ≠ cap := by
++              intro h
++              have hv := congrArg Fin.val h
++              rw [hviEx, hvcap] at hv
++              have := e.isLt
++              rw [hkC, hup] at hv
++              omega
++            show _ = (if iEx e = iBuf then (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℤ) else if iEx e = cap then
++              (bt.length : ℤ) else if iEx e ∈ Sx then (bt.length : ℤ) else 0)
++            rw [if_neg h3, if_neg h4, if_pos heS]
++          · have h1 := (embedSilentCfg_unselected ιC cap (fun _ _ => none) (fun _ => 0) [] []
++              CC i (hnr i hib hiS) hic).2
++            refine h1.trans ?_
++            show _ = (if i = iBuf then (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℤ) else if i = cap then
++              (bt.length : ℤ) else if i ∈ Sx then (bt.length : ℤ) else 0)
++            rw [if_neg hib, if_neg hic, if_neg hiS]
++    exact ⟨hend.trans (Cfg.ext rfl hip (funext htap) (funext hhd) hout), rfl, hcut1⟩
++  -- phase 2: rewind the code buffer's head to the word start
++  have hs2 : A2.runFrom (E1.mapState fun _ => RewindPhase.start) T2 = E2 ∧
++      E2.state = some RewindPhase.done ∧

++      rfl

++              (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℕ) + p) = FinTM.bufferTape u p

++      push_cast
++      ring
++    obtain ⟨hrun2, hcut2, -⟩ := rewindTM_run_ofCfg iBuf u (2 * bt.length + 2)
++      (by omega) (E1.mapState fun _ => RewindPhase.start) hst hhu
++    refine ⟨hrun2.trans ?_, rfl, hcut2⟩
++    refine Cfg.ext rfl ?_ ?_ ?_ ?_
++    · exact Cfg.mapState_inputPos (fun _ => RewindPhase.start) E1
++    · funext i q

++          ((2 * bt.length + 2 : ℕ) : ℤ)) i = E2.workTapePos i
++      rw [Function.update_apply]

++      · rw [if_pos hib, hib, Cfg.mapState_workTapePos]

++            (2 * bt.length + 2 : ℕ) =

++        ring

++          (if i = cap then (bt.length : ℤ) else if i ∈ Sx then (bt.length : ℤ) else 0)
++        rw [if_neg hib]
++    · exact Cfg.mapState_output (fun _ => RewindPhase.start) E1
++  -- phase 3: clear the code buffer
++  have hs3 : A3.runFrom (E2.mapState fun _ => SweepPhase.sweep) T3 = E3 ∧
++      E3.state = some SweepPhase.done ∧
++      ∀ v < T3, (A3.runFrom (E2.mapState fun _ => SweepPhase.sweep) v).state ≠

++      rfl

++        (if i = cap then FinTM.bufferTape bt else if i ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt
++          else fun _ => none) q
++      rw [if_neg hib]
++  -- phase 4: the guided bank erase, which also rewinds the capture head
++  have hs4 : A4.runFrom (E3.mapState fun _ => (0 : Fin 3)) T4 = E4 ∧
++      E4.state = some 2 ∧
++      ∀ v < T4, (A4.runFrom (E3.mapState fun _ => (0 : Fin 3)) v).state ≠ some 2 := by
++    have htape4 : ∀ i, i ∈ Sx ∨ i = cap →

++    obtain ⟨hrun4, hcut4⟩ := un_bank_run Sx cap hcapSx bt.length (fun _ => bt)
++      (fun _ _ => rfl) (E3.mapState fun _ => (0 : Fin 3))

++      show (if i ∈ Sx then (fun _ => none) else

++        rw [if_neg hic]
++      · rw [if_neg hiS, Cfg.mapState_workTapes]
++        show (if i = cap then FinTM.bufferTape bt else if i ∈ Sx then FinTM.bufferTape bt

++        rw [if_neg (fun h => hig (Or.inr h)), if_neg (fun h => hig (Or.inl h))]
++  -- phase 5: the expansion of the captured budget word into the unary block
++  have hd5' : Nat.ofDigits 2 (bt.map Bool.toNat) = t :=
++    (counterValue_eq bt).symm.trans (counterValue_bits t)
++  have hs5 : A5.runFrom (E4.mapState fun _ => CounterLoopState.dec FlagPhase.run) T5 = E5 ∧

++      ∀ v < T5, (A5.runFrom (E4.mapState fun _ => CounterLoopState.dec FlagPhase.run) v).state ≠
++        some CounterLoopState.done := by
++    -- the junction into the counter host's layout
++    have hJ5 : (E4.mapState fun _ => CounterLoopState.dec FlagPhase.run) =

++        rfl

++        · rw [Fin.lastCases_castSucc]
++          rw [Cfg.mapState_workTapePos]
++    have hrow := expandTM_run_ofCfg (x := z) iBlkb [true]

++        Cfg (K + MC.k) Bool (Fin 2) z) rfl bt (FinTM.bufferTape bt) 0

++      · funext j

++        · rw [Fin.lastCases_last]

++            tp5 (Fin.last (K + MC.k)) q
++          show (if 0 ≤ q ∧ q < 0 + (bt.length : ℤ) then some true else FinTM.bufferTape bt q) =

++              FinTM.bufferTape (List.replicate t [true]).flatten (q - 0)
++            else tp4 (Fin.castSucc j') q) = tp5 (Fin.castSucc j') q
++          have hne : Fin.castSucc j' ≠ cap := by
++            intro h
++            have hv := congrArg Fin.val h
++            rw [Fin.coe_castSucc, hvcap] at hv
++            have := j'.isLt
++            omega
++          by_cases hjb : j' = iBlkb
++          · rw [hjb]
++            show (if iBlkb = iBlkb ∧ (0 : ℤ) ≤ q ∧ q < 0 + ((t * [true].length : ℕ) : ℤ) then
++                FinTM.bufferTape (List.replicate t [true]).flatten (q - 0)
++              else tp4 (Fin.castSucc iBlkb) q) = tp5 (Fin.castSucc iBlkb) q
++            have htp5v : tp5 (Fin.castSucc iBlkb) q = blk q := by
++              show (if Fin.castSucc iBlkb = cap then capW else if Fin.castSucc iBlkb = ι jR
++                then blk else fun _ => none) q = blk q
++              rw [if_neg (hjb ▸ hne),

+            t ≤ s.length →

+              ∃ T,

+    ∀ (w : List Bool) (ct : ℤ → Option Bool) (cp : ℤ),
+      (∀ (p : ℤ), -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → ct (cp + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →
+        ∀ (d : ℕ),
+          Nat.ofDigits 2 (List.map Bool.toNat w) = d →
+            Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                  (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)
+                  (d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1)) =
+                Turing.counterLoopCfg (some Turing.CounterLoopState.done)
+                  { state := c.state, inputPos := c.inputPos,
+                    workTapes := fun j q =>
+                      if j = i ∧ c.workTapePos j ≤ q ∧ q < c.workTapePos j + ↑(d * pat.length) then
+                        Turing.FinTM.bufferTape (List.replicate d pat).flatten (q - c.workTapePos j)
+                      else c.workTapes j q,
+                    workTapePos := Function.update c.workTapePos i (c.workTapePos i + ↑(d * pat.length)),
+                    output := c.output }
+                  (fun q => if cp ≤ q ∧ q < cp + ↑w.length then some true else ct q) cp ∧
+              (∀ t < d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1),
+                  (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                          (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)
+                          t).state ≠
+                      some Turing.CounterLoopState.done ∧
+                    (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                          (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)
+                          t).state ≠
+                      some Turing.CounterLoopState.escape) ∧
+                (∀ t ≤ d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1),
+                    (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                            (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct cp)
+                            t).workTapePos
+                        (Fin.last k) ∈
+                      Finset.Icc (cp - 1) (cp + ↑w.length)) ∧
+                  ∀ t ≤ d * (pat.length + 1) + Turing.counterOverhead w (d + 1),
+                    ∀ (j : Fin k),
+                      if j = i then
+                        (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                                (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct
+                                  cp)
+                                t).workTapePos
+                            j.castSucc ∈
+                          Finset.Icc (c.workTapePos j) (c.workTapePos j + ↑(d * pat.length))
+                      else
+                        (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                                (Turing.counterLoopCfg (some (Turing.CounterLoopState.dec Turing.FlagPhase.run)) c ct
+                                  cp)
+                                t).workTapePos
+                            j.castSucc =
+                          c.workTapePos j
+@Turing.clearTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (w : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SweepPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SweepPhase.sweep →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ), -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (2 * w.length + 2) =
+          { state := some Turing.SweepPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos,
+            workTapes := fun j q =>
+              if j = i ∧ d.workTapePos j ≤ q ∧ q < d.workTapePos j + ↑w.length then none else d.workTapes j q,
+            workTapePos := d.workTapePos, output := d.output } ∧
+        (∀ t < 2 * w.length + 2, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.SweepPhase.done) ∧
+          ∀ (j : Fin k),
+            ∀ t ≤ 2 * w.length + 2,
+              if j = i then
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                  Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j - 1) (d.workTapePos j + ↑w.length)
+              else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
+@Turing.skipBackTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (u : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SeekPhase.run →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ),
+        -1 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑u.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i - ↑u.length - 1 + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape u p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (u.length + 3) =
+          { state := some Turing.SeekPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos, workTapes := d.workTapes,
+            workTapePos := Function.update d.workTapePos i (d.workTapePos i - ↑u.length - 1), output := d.output } ∧
+        (∀ t < u.length + 3, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.SeekPhase.done) ∧
+          ∀ (j : Fin k),
+            ∀ t ≤ u.length + 3,
+              if j = i then
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                  Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j - ↑u.length - 2) (d.workTapePos j)
+              else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
+@Turing.seamCompTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S₁ : Type u_1} {S₂ : Type u_2} {Symbol : Type u_3} {x : List Symbol}
+  [inst : DecidableEq S₁] (M₁ : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S₁) (exit : S₁) (M₂ : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S₂)
+  (entry : S₂) {c₀ c₁ : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S₁ x} {c₃ : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S₂ x} {T₁ T₂ : ℕ},
+  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c₀ T₁ = c₁ →
+    c₁.state = some exit →
+      (∀ t < T₁, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c₀ t).state ≠ some exit) →
+        Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => entry) c₁) T₂ = c₃ →
+          Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl c₀) (T₁ + 1 + T₂) = Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inr c₃
+@Turing.seamReleaseTM_firstReturn : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol}
+  (M : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol S) (anchor : S) {c c' : Turing.Cfg k Symbol S x} {T : ℕ},
+  c.state = some anchor →
+    0 < T →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T = c' →
+        c'.state = some anchor →
+          (∀ (t : ℕ), 0 < t → t < T → (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c t).state ≠ some anchor) →
+            Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => Sum.inl ()) c) T =
+                Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inr c' ∧
+              ∀ t < T,
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => Sum.inl ()) c) t).state ≠
+                  some (Sum.inr anchor)
+@Turing.phaseHostTM_run : ∀ {k : ℕ} {Γ I : Type} {Ph : I → Type} (M : (i : I) → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Γ (Ph i))
+  (q0 : (i : I) × Ph i) (red : (i : I) → Ph i → Option ((i : I) × Ph i)) (i : I) {x : List Γ}
+  (c c' : Turing.Cfg k Γ (Ph i) x) (T : ℕ),
+  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T = c' →
+    ∀ (s' : Ph i),
+      c'.state = some s' →
+        ∀ (q' : (i : I) × Ph i),
+          red i s' = some q' →
+            (∀ u < T, ∀ (s : Ph i), (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c u).state = some s → red i s = none) →
+              (∀ u ≤ T,
+                  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (Sigma.mk i) c) u =
+                    Turing.Cfg.mapState (Sigma.mk i) (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c u)) ∧
+                Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom (Turing.Cfg.mapState (Sigma.mk i) c) (T + 1) =
+                  Turing.Cfg.mapState (fun x => q') c'

+Turing.FinTM.bufferedSecondCfg_workTapes_cast : ∀ (M₁ M₂ : Turing.FinTM Bool) {x y : List Bool}

+  (M₁.bufferedSecondCfg M₂ c b p tapes heads).workTapes i =
+    Turing.FinTM.tapeBlocks tapes (Turing.FinTM.bufferTape y) c.workTapes (Fin.cast ⋯ i)
