+@Turing.embedSilentRetTM : {m k : ℕ} →
+  {S : Type u_1} →
+    {Symbol : Type u_2} →
+      (Fin m ↪ Fin k) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM m Symbol S → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol (S ⊕ Unit)
+@Turing.embedSilentRetTM_run : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k),
+  ∀ cap ∉ Set.range ⇑ι,
+    ∀ (M : Turing.MultiTapeTM m Symbol S) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ)
+      (pre out₀ : List Symbol) (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x) (T : ℕ),
+      c.state ≠ none →
+        (∀ t < T, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c t).state ≠ none) →
+          (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T).state = none →
+            (∀ t < T,
+                Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                    (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) t =
+                  Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl
+                    (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c t))) ∧
+              (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                    (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) T =
+                  have __src := Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c T);
+                  { state := some (Sum.inr ()), inputPos := __src.inputPos, workTapes := __src.workTapes,
+                    workTapePos := __src.workTapePos, output := __src.output }) ∧
+                ∀ t < T,
+                  (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom
+                        (Turing.Cfg.mapState Sum.inl (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c)) t).state ≠
+                    some (Sum.inr ())
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg : {m k : ℕ} →
+  {S : Type u_1} →
+    {Symbol : Type u_2} →
+      {x : List Symbol} →
+        (Fin m ↪ Fin k) →
+          Fin k →
+            (Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) →
+              (Fin k → ℤ) → List Symbol → List Symbol → Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x → Turing.Cfg k Symbol S x
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg_unselected : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k)
+  (cap : Fin k) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ) (pre out₀ : List Symbol)
+  (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x),
+  ∀ j ∉ Set.range ⇑ι,
+    j ≠ cap →
+      (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).workTapes j = tapes j ∧
+        (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).workTapePos j = heads j
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg_inputPos : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k)
+  (cap : Fin k) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ) (pre out₀ : List Symbol)
+  (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x), (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).inputPos = c.inputPos
+@Turing.embedSilentCfg_output : ∀ {m k : ℕ} {S : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {x : List Symbol} (ι : Fin m ↪ Fin k)
+  (cap : Fin k) (tapes : Fin k → ℤ → Option Symbol) (heads : Fin k → ℤ) (pre out₀ : List Symbol)
+  (c : Turing.Cfg m Symbol S x), (Turing.embedSilentCfg ι cap tapes heads pre out₀ c).output = out₀
+@Turing.MultiTapeTM.exists_first_halt : ∀ {k : ℕ} {Symbol : Type u_1} {State : Type u_2} {input : List Symbol}
+  (tm : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol State) (cfg : Turing.Cfg k Symbol State input) {t : ℕ},
+  (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg t).state = none →
+    ∃ u ≤ t,
+      (∀ v < u, ¬(Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg v).Halted) ∧
+        Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg u = Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg t
+@Turing.skipTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (w : List Symbol)
+  (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase x),
+  d.state = some Turing.SeekPhase.run →
+    (∀ (p : ℤ), 0 ≤ p → p ≤ ↑w.length → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w p) →
+      Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (w.length + 1) =
+          { state := some Turing.SeekPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos, workTapes := d.workTapes,
+            workTapePos := Function.update d.workTapePos i (d.workTapePos i + ↑w.length + 1), output := d.output } ∧
+        (∀ t < w.length + 1, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.SeekPhase.done) ∧
+          ∀ (j : Fin k),
+            ∀ t ≤ w.length + 1,
+              if j = i then
+                (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                  Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j) (d.workTapePos j + ↑w.length + 1)
+              else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
+@Turing.clearTM : {Symbol : Type u_1} → (k : ℕ) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol Turing.SweepPhase
+@Turing.skipBackTM : {Symbol : Type u_1} → (k : ℕ) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase
+@Turing.skipTM : {Symbol : Type u_1} → (k : ℕ) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol Turing.SeekPhase
+@Turing.FinTM.rewind_heads : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type} {x : List Bool} (tm : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Bool S) (start scan : S)
+  (dest : Option S),
+  (∀ (inp : Option Bool) (work : Fin k → Option Bool),
+      tm.tr start inp work = Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.neg (some scan)) →
+    (∀ (inp : Option Bool) (work : Fin k → Option Bool),
+        tm.tr scan inp work =
+          match inp with
+          | some val => Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.neg (some scan)
+          | none => Turing.FinTM.controlAction SignType.pos dest) →
+      ∀ (c : Turing.Cfg k Bool S x),
+        c.state = some start →
+          ∃ r ≤ ↑c.inputPos + 2,
+            Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c r =
+                { state := dest, inputPos := 1, workTapes := c.workTapes, workTapePos := c.workTapePos,
+                  output := c.output } ∧
+              ∀ u ≤ r, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom c u).workTapePos = c.workTapePos
+@Turing.FinTM.controlAction : {k : ℕ} → {S : Type} → SignType → Option S → Turing.Action k Bool S
+@Turing.FinTM.controlAction_apply : ∀ {k : ℕ} {S : Type} {x : List Bool} (cfg : Turing.Cfg k Bool S x) (m : SignType)
+  (q : Option S),
+  (Turing.FinTM.controlAction m q).apply cfg =
+    { state := q, inputPos := Turing.moveInputPos cfg.inputPos m, workTapes := cfg.workTapes,
+      workTapePos := cfg.workTapePos, output := cfg.output }
+@Turing.FinTM.bufferTape_eq : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} (w : List Symbol) (z : ℤ),
+  Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w z = if 0 ≤ z then w[z.toNat]? else none
+@Turing.FinTM.bufferTape_of_neg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} (w : List Symbol) {z : ℤ},
+  z < 0 → Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w z = none
+@Turing.FinTM.bufferTape_nat : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} (w : List Symbol) (i : ℕ), Turing.FinTM.bufferTape w ↑i = w[i]?
+Turing.length_pairEncode : ∀ (x y : List Bool), (Turing.pairEncode x y).length = 2 * x.length + 2 + y.length
+Turing.pairEncode_eq : ∀ (x α : List Bool),
+  Turing.pairEncode x α = List.flatMap (fun b => [b, b]) x ++ [false, true] ++ α
+Turing.pairDecode_pairEncode : ∀ (x α : List Bool), Turing.pairDecode (Turing.pairEncode x α) = some (x, α)

+  (Turing.Cfg.init q₀ input).state = some q₀

+  (Turing.Cfg.init q₀ input).inputPos = 1
+@Turing.Cfg.init_workTapes : ∀ {k : ℕ} {State : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} (q₀ : State) (input : List Symbol)
+  (x : Fin k) (x_1 : ℤ), (Turing.Cfg.init q₀ input).workTapes x x_1 = none

+  (Turing.Cfg.init q₀ input).output = []
+@Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom_succ_eq_step' : ∀ {k : ℕ} {State : Type u_1} {Symbol : Type u_2} {input : List Symbol}
+  {tm : Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol State} {cfg : Turing.Cfg k Symbol State input} {t : ℕ},
+  Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg (t + 1) = Turing.MultiTapeTM.step (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom cfg t)
+Turing.RewindPhase : Type
+@Turing.rewindTM : {Symbol : Type u_1} → (k : ℕ) → Fin k → Turing.MultiTapeTM k Symbol Turing.RewindPhase
+@Turing.rewindTM_run_ofCfg : ∀ {Symbol : Type u_1} {k : ℕ} {x : List Symbol} (i : Fin k) (u : List Symbol),
+  ∀ n ≤ u.length,
+    ∀ (d : Turing.Cfg k Symbol Turing.RewindPhase x),
+      d.state = some Turing.RewindPhase.start →
+        (∀ (p : ℤ), -1 ≤ p → p < ↑n → d.workTapes i (d.workTapePos i - ↑n + p) = Turing.FinTM.bufferTape u p) →
+          Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d (n + 2) =
+              { state := some Turing.RewindPhase.done, inputPos := d.inputPos, workTapes := d.workTapes,
+                workTapePos := Function.update d.workTapePos i (d.workTapePos i - ↑n), output := d.output } ∧
+            (∀ t < n + 2, (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).state ≠ some Turing.RewindPhase.done) ∧
+              ∀ (j : Fin k),
+                ∀ t ≤ n + 2,
+                  if j = i then
+                    (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j ∈
+                      Finset.Icc (d.workTapePos j - ↑n - 1) (d.workTapePos j)
+                  else (Turing.MultiTapeTM.runFrom d t).workTapePos j = d.workTapePos j
